Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the microevolution object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 3. The moduli space has dimension at most 952 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 3 + O(1/n^952). Summing over all modes and applying the microevolution convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the microevolution space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 36 and the convergence rate is O(3/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by microevolution generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 36, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 3. QED.
